- **exchange-order-fund**: Fund an unfunded SELL order with its AIT escrow lock. Reads the funding instruction from my-orders (escrow address, required AIT, sell_quote payload) and sends the lock TRANSFER from this node's own wallet — the sender must be the order owner (stranger locks are refunded, never credited). --print emits the equivalent 'transactions send' line without broadcasting anything. Operator note (CLI-1/CLI- ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/exchange-order-fund.md))
- **exchange-order-my**: Show this node's orders and next exchange nonce (read-only). ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/exchange-order-my.md))
- **exchange-order-place**: Sign and place an AIT/ETH order. Under settlement the response carries the funding instruction — rendered as a hint above the JSON. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/exchange-order-place.md))
- **exchange-prepay-deposit**: Deposit Sepolia ETH into this node's prepaid exchange balance. Sends to the hub's bridge deposit address with the ``EXCHANGE_DEPOSIT:&lt;node address&gt;`` input-data tag; the deposit monitor records it 'exchange_prepay' and the settlement monitor credits the prepaid balance (and auto-funds any open unfunded BUYs, in id order). ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/exchange-prepay-deposit.md))
